					limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_IRQ_iran_central_administration_invoice_frozen } }

					multiply_variable = { COLDWAR_IRQ_iran_central_administration_invoice = 0.025 }
					set_country_flag = COLDWAR_IRQ_iran_central_administration_invoice_frozen
				}

					limit = {
						tag = IRQ 399 = { is_owned_and_controlled_by = IRQ NOT = { is_core_of = IRQ } } has_country_flag = COLDWAR_IRQ_iran_provincial_settlement_implemented
						check_variable = { var = treasury value = COLDWAR_IRQ_iran_central_administration_invoice compare = greater_than_or_equals }
					}
					set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = COLDWAR_IRQ_iran_central_administration_invoice }
					multiply_temp_variable = { treasury_change = -1 }
					modify_treasury_effect = yes

					set_country_flag = COLDWAR_IRQ_iran_central_administration_package_earned
					399 = { add_compliance = 6 }
					set_country_flag = COLDWAR_IRQ_iran_central_administration_implemented
					clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_IRQ_iran_central_administration_pending

				}
				else = {
					set_country_flag = COLDWAR_IRQ_iran_central_administration_pending
				}
			}
		}
		ai_will_do = { base = 1 }
